<h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Plugin branding:</strong> package your plugin icon at <code>assets/icon.png</code> instead of a top-level manifest URL; OpenClaw loads the packaged image without a network request, and missing or invalid icons do not prevent the plugin from loading. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5274628315" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131510" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131510/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131510">#131510</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Patrick-Erichsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen">@Patrick-Erichsen</a>.</li>
<li><strong>GPT-6 Astra async tools and steering:</strong> on OpenAI Platform API-key routes using the built-in OpenClaw runtime and official Responses endpoint, run direct function tools asynchronously, steer active responses over cached WebSockets, and retain request prefixes when changing reasoning effort under the documented configuration; keep steering history and encrypted reasoning through continuations. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5345641743" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138046" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138046/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138046">#138046</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5351069089" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138434" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138434/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138434">#138434</a>)</li>
<li><strong>GPT-6 Astra reasoning:</strong> keep supported reasoning and sampling settings correct when catalog metadata is absent, add <code>/think ultra</code> orchestration for OpenClaw and native Codex runtimes, and retain configured Azure deployment capabilities. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5353231838" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138626" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138626/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138626">#138626</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5354536320" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138739" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138739/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138739">#138739</a>)</li>
<li><strong>macOS browser sign-in:</strong> connect saved Gateways through personal Cloudflare Access browser sign-in, open the Mac app from the Gateway website, and restore the selected Gateway after app restart while keeping accounts and queued messages separate. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5354098596" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138698" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138698/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138698">#138698</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5344352793" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137916" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137916/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137916">#137916</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5354584375" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138745" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138745/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138745">#138745</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5344538207" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137933" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137933/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137933">#137933</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Experimental plugin UI:</strong> enable Settings → Labs → Custom plugin UI to let plugins contribute Control UI pages, panels, and session actions or customize the composer and workspace. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5308783929" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134943" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134943/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134943">#134943</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Experimental Apple Watch Talk:</strong> opt into standalone Talk on Watch with Gateway-owned tools and transcripts while retaining the existing iPhone-mediated Talk to Claw flow; standalone realtime audio requires UDP connectivity. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5318940960" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/135808" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/135808/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/135808">#135808</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Slack rich replies:</strong> guide agents to use supported Block Kit layouts for proactive Slack replies. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5355927830" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138896" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138896/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138896">#138896</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Patrick-Erichsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen">@Patrick-Erichsen</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Personal connected accounts:</strong> add provider accounts from Settings → Profile → Connected accounts or the CLI, choose an account for new chats or one existing chat, and keep existing session selections when your default changes. <h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Question answers:</strong> recover lost answer confirmations without sending the same input through ordinary steering again, and report uncertainty visibly while preserving independently accepted work. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5298461126" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134003" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134003/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134003">#134003</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/edenfunf/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/edenfunf">@edenfunf</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Doctor database diagnostics:</strong> report the concrete SQLite error for a stable malformed database instead of incorrectly blaming concurrent changes, while preserving the source database and its sidecars. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5358414970" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139136" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139136/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139136">#139136</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/vincentkoc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/vincentkoc">@vincentkoc</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Plugin SDK code ranges:</strong> accept positional <code>CodeRegion</code> inputs containing only <code>start</code> and <code>end</code> again; consumers needing parser metadata should retain the inferred <code>findCodeRegions</code> result. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5340545346" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137509" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137509/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137509">#137509</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5340551684" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137510" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137510/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137510">#137510</a>)</li>
<li><strong>HTTP proxy safety:</strong> update HTTP dependencies and keep destination DNS checks in effect when requests bypass a configured proxy. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5357705297" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139056" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139056/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139056">#139056</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Transcript rewrites:</strong> leave live history intact when a durable tail rewrite fails and retain the admitted user input through repeated branch rewrites. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5350844380" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138412" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138412/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138412">#138412</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5356751578" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138967" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138967/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138967">#138967</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/fuller-stack-dev/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev">@fuller-stack-dev</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Session cleanup:</strong> preserve other agents’ plugin state on session deletion, keep case-sensitive peer identifiers distinct, and prevent canceled queued cleanup from erasing newly restored plugin state. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5118785240" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/121984" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/121984/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/121984">#121984</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5356251085" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138925" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138925/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138925">#138925</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5119515342" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/122006" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/122006/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/122006">#122006</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5356592803" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138957" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138957/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138957">#138957</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/RamiNoodle733/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/RamiNoodle733">@RamiNoodle733</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>, and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/yunligou711-commits/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/yunligou711-commits">@yunligou711-commits</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Skill Workshop backups:</strong> refuse incomplete directory snapshots and verify retained originals before restore so deep user edits are not overwritten by an incomplete view. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5345918654" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138082" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138082/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138082">#138082</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Pending child launches:</strong> stop pending native and ACP child launches when their originating parent authority closes, while preserving ownership already transferred to accepted children. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5357265717" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139001" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139001/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139001">#139001</a>. <li><strong>Provider sign-in:</strong> keep the active plugin registry intact while discovering provider sign-in methods. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5355420519" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138838" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138838/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138838">#138838</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Automation replies:</strong> keep automation and heartbeat completions attached to their originating conversation and topic while retaining the isolated execution session and its policy. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5292572218" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133323" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133323/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133323">#133323</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Marvinthebored/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Marvinthebored">@Marvinthebored</a>, <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Peetiegonzalez/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez">@Peetiegonzalez</a>, and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>.</li>

<h3>Upcoming deprecations</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Plugin SDK context aliases:</strong> deprecated untrusted-named prompt-context aliases become eligible for removal on or after September 8, 2026. </ul>

<h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Plugin branding:</strong> package your plugin icon at <code>assets/icon.png</code> instead of a top-level manifest URL; OpenClaw loads the packaged image without a network request, and missing or invalid icons do not prevent the plugin from loading. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5274628315" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/131510" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131510/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131510">#131510</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Patrick-Erichsen/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen">@Patrick-Erichsen</a>.</li>
<li><strong>GPT-6 Astra async tools and steering:</strong> on OpenAI Platform API-key routes using the built-in OpenClaw runtime and official Responses endpoint, run direct function tools asynchronously, steer active responses over cached WebSockets, and retain request prefixes when changing reasoning effort under the documented configuration; keep steering history and encrypted reasoning through continuations. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5345641743" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138046" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138046/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138046">#138046</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5351069089" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138434" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138434/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138434">#138434</a>)</li>
<li><strong>GPT-6 Astra reasoning:</strong> keep supported reasoning and sampling settings correct when catalog metadata is absent, add <code>/think ultra</code> orchestration for OpenClaw and native Codex runtimes, and retain configured Azure deployment capabilities. <h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Question answers:</strong> recover lost answer confirmations without sending the same input through ordinary steering again, and report uncertainty visibly while preserving independently accepted work. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5298461126" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134003" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134003/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134003">#134003</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/edenfunf/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/edenfunf">@edenfunf</a> and <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/obviyus/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/obviyus">@obviyus</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Doctor database diagnostics:</strong> report the concrete SQLite error for a stable malformed database instead of incorrectly blaming concurrent changes, while preserving the source database and its sidecars. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5358414970" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139136" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139136/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139136">#139136</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/vincentkoc/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/vincentkoc">@vincentkoc</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Plugin SDK code ranges:</strong> accept positional <code>CodeRegion</code> inputs containing only <code>start</code> and <code>end</code> again; consumers needing parser metadata should retain the inferred <code>findCodeRegions</code> result. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5340545346" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137509" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137509/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137509">#137509</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5340551684" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/137510" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137510/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137510">#137510</a>)</li>
<li><strong>HTTP proxy safety:</strong> update HTTP dependencies and keep destination DNS checks in effect when requests bypass a configured proxy. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5357705297" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/139056" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139056/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/139056">#139056</a>)</li>
<li><strong>Transcript rewrites:</strong> leave live history intact when a durable tail rewrite fails and retain the admitted user input through repeated branch rewrites. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5350844380" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138412" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138412/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138412">#138412</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5356751578" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/138967" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138967/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138967">#138967</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/fuller-stack-dev/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev">@fuller-stack-dev</a>.</li>

<h3>Upcoming deprecations</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Plugin SDK context aliases:</strong> deprecated untrusted-named prompt-context aliases become eligible for removal on or after September 8, 2026. </ul>
